DESIGN.md

# Scale Shift

## Concept
A simple tap toggles the player’s dot between a tiny and a large radius. The tunnel’s gaps are sized for one radius or the other, so the player must switch sizes at the right moment to thread each opening, creating a rhythmic challenge of timing and anticipation.

## Player
The avatar is a single circular dot that automatically moves forward through a scrolling tunnel. The player only controls its radius, toggling between “small” and “large” states with a tap or click.

## Core loop
Every 1–3 seconds a new tunnel segment approaches, its opening sized for either the small or the large dot. The dot continuously advances; the player watches the upcoming gap, decides whether to stay the current size or toggle, and hits the input. The game instantly applies the new radius, and the dot either passes cleanly or collides. Successful passes repeat the pattern, while difficulty subtly ramps up.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Mismatched gaps** – tunnel openings that only fit one radius; entering with the wrong size results in an immediate collision.
- **Moving barriers** – occasional rotating spikes or sliding blocks that require the correct radius to slip past.
- **Speed increase** – the tunnel scrolls faster over time, reducing reaction windows.

## Score
- +1 point for each gap successfully traversed.
- Bonus point for toggling within a tight timing window (e.g., less than 0.2 s before the gap reaches the dot).
- The current score is always displayed.

## Win and lose
- **Lose:** Collision with a tunnel wall or moving barrier triggers a “Game Over” overlay displaying the final score and restart instructions.
- **Win:** Survive a predefined number of gaps (e.g., 50) or reach a distance threshold, triggering a celebratory “You Win!” screen with a score summary.

## Feel
A fast‑paced, reflex‑driven arcade experience with a neon‑lit tunnel, dark background, and crisp synth stabs on each toggle. The game feels tense yet rewarding, encouraging a rhythm of anticipation and rapid response.

## Why it's fun
The satisfying “just‑in‑time” toggle that lets you slip through a seemingly impossible hole makes you grin, curse, and want to try again.

TASKS.md

show
1. Create HTML scaffold and meta.json — build base page with header, description, controls list, canvas, restart button, stub script block, and generate a matching meta.json per CONVENTIONS.md.
2. Initialize game state objects — define player (position, radius states), hazards array, score, phase, and constants (SAFE_RADIUS, GAP_SMALL, GAP_LARGE, etc.) at the top of the script.
3. Implement animation loop — write startLoop with cancelAnimationFrame handling, try/catch around loop body, clear canvas each frame.
4. Set up input system — add keyboard listeners to track a Set of pressed keys, handle R key for restart, click/tap to toggle radius, and restart button click, all calling a common reset function.
5. Implement player radius toggle — on input toggle the player between small and large radius, update player state accordingly.
6. Add forward motion and tunnel progression — auto‑advance the player forward through the tunnel each frame and update tunnel offset based on dt.
7. Spawn tunnel segments and hazards — create spawn timer logic that generates new tunnel gaps sized for the current radius, respecting SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns too close at start.
8. Detect collisions — compare player radius to tunnel gap boundaries each frame; on collision set phase to "gameover" and stop gameplay.
9. Implement scoring — increase score on each successfully traversed gap, draw the score at the top‑left of the canvas.
10. Render game elements — clear canvas, draw background, tunnel walls, player dot (high‑contrast), and HUD elements.
11. Add game‑over overlay and restart flow — dim background, display "Game Over" with final score and restart instructions; ensure R key and restart button both reset the game.
12. Expose observable contract — populate window.__game each frame with state ("menu"|"playing"|"gameover"), score, player position/visibility, and a one‑sentence objective.
13. Integrate audio cues — lazy‑init an AudioContext in a beep() helper, wrap in try/catch, and trigger sounds on radius toggle, scoring events, and game‑over.

<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Scale Shift</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Toggle the dot’s radius to match tunnel gaps as they scroll by, threading through openings in rhythm.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Tap or click to toggle size</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
    // === CONFIG =============================================================
    // Game constants
    const PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL = 5;
    const PLAYER_RADIUS_LARGE = 12;
    const SAFE_RADIUS = 200;
    const GAP_SMALL = PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL; // gap size for small radius
    const GAP_LARGE = PLAYER_RADIUS_LARGE; // gap size for large radius
    const PLAYER_SPEED = 200; // forward speed in px/sec

    // === STATE =============================================================
    const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const restartBtn = document.getElementById('restart');
    let loopId = null;
    let keys = new Set();
    let audioCtx = null;
    // Game state variables
    let player = { x: canvas.width/2, y: canvas.height/2, radius: PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL, targetRadius: PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL, visible: true };
    let hazards = [];
    let score = 0;
    let phase = "menu"; // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"
    let lastTime = performance.now();
    let spawnTimer = 0;

    // === RESET =============================================================
    function reset() {
      // Initialize or reinitialize game state
      player.x = canvas.width/2;
      player.y = canvas.height/2;
      player.radius = PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL;
      player.targetRadius = PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL;
      player.visible = true;
      hazards = [];
      score = 0;
      phase = "playing";
      // Clear canvas
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      // Draw player placeholder
      ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fill();
      // Publish initial observable state
      publishState();
      startLoop();
    }

    // === LOOP/UPDATE =============================================================
    // Animation loop handling
    function startLoop() {
      // Cancel any existing animation frame to avoid stacking loops
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          // Clear canvas each frame
          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
          // Basic render placeholder: draw player dot
          ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
          // Publish observable state each frame
          publishState();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("loop error", err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    // === RENDER =============================================================
    // (to be implemented in later tasks)

    // === INPUT =============================================================
    // Keyboard tracking
    document.addEventListener('keydown', ev => {
      if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
      // Toggle radius on space
      if (ev.key === ' ') {
        player.targetRadius = (player.targetRadius === PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL) ? PLAYER_RADIUS_LARGE : PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL;
        player.radius = player.targetRadius;
      }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener('keyup', ev => keys.delete(ev.key));
    // Mouse / touch input for radius toggle
    // Click or tap on the canvas toggles the player's radius
    canvas.addEventListener('click', () => {
      player.targetRadius = (player.targetRadius === PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL) ? PLAYER_RADIUS_LARGE : PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL;
      player.radius = player.targetRadius;
    });
    canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', ev => {
      ev.preventDefault();
      player.targetRadius = (player.targetRadius === PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL) ? PLAYER_RADIUS_LARGE : PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL;
      player.radius = player.targetRadius;
    }, { passive: false });
    // Restart button
    restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);

    // === AUDIO (lazy) =============================================================
    // (to be implemented in later tasks)

    // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
    function publishState() {
      window.__game = {
        state: phase,
        score: score,
        player: { x: player.x, y: player.y, visible: player.visible },
        objective: 'Toggle the dot’s radius to match tunnel gaps as they scroll by.'
      };
    }

    // === BOOT ===============================================================
    // Initial setup
    reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

Concept: The player taps to toggle a dot's radius between small and large, each tunnel gap is sized for a specific radius; must switch sizes at right moments to thread openings. Satisfying because of timing and binary decision making, toggling rhythm.

Player: The player controls a singular dot (or simple circle) that moves forward through a scrolling tunnel automatically; the only interaction is tapping / clicking (or space) to toggle its size between two radius states (tiny/large). Verbs: toggle size, maybe start (implied), restart (but restart is part of UI not design). Keep abstract: "toggle" is the main verb.

Core loop: At a regular interval (maybe each gap appears about 1-3 seconds) the tunnel provides a gap sized for one of the two radii; the dot moves forward automatically; the player watches upcoming gap and decides whether to stay small or become large, tapping to toggle at appropriate timing; the visual feedback shows the gap approaching; the player feels tension as they anticipate and must react precisely; the loop continues as obstacles generate, speed increasing gradually.

Hazards / Obstacles: Tunnel walls with variable gap sizes, exactly one fits small radius, another fits large radius; optionally moving obstacles like rotating spikes inside the tunnel that require correct size to pass; perhaps "dead zones" where the tunnel narrows further; but the main hazard is mismatched gap.

Score: Earn points for each gap successfully traversed, or for distance traveled, but to avoid time-only, maybe each successful pass increments score; extra points for near misses (toggling at the last moment) maybe.

Win and lose: Player loses when the dot collides with a tunnel wall because radius does not match gap; losing shows game over overlay; Player wins if they survive a set number of gaps (e.g., 50) or reach a distance/time threshold; win triggers victory celebration.

Feel: Fast-paced rhythmic arcade; neon aesthetic with tunnel; sound: minimal chiptune pulses on toggle and collision; vibe: tense, reflexive.

Why it's fun: The moment of "just in time" toggling, making you feel a rush as you squeeze through an impossibly tight hole.

## Hazards / Obstacles

Never make the player wait.\n92: 2. **Clear feedback** — the player always knows when something happened. Use color flashes, particles, brief screen shake, score popups, or sound on every meaningful event (hit, score, miss, death).\n93: 3. **Win/lose state** — the game has a clear end condition. On lose, draw \"Game Over\" + final score + restart instructions on the canvas. On win, celebrate (color burst, \"You Win!\" overlay).\n94: 4. **Restart without page reload** — both pressing R AND clicking the `<button id=\"restart\">` reset the game cleanly via the same code path. Mention both in `controls` (e.g. \"Press R or click Restart\"). On restart you MUST `cancelAnimationFrame` any pending loop before starting a new one (otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game eventually slows to a crawl).\n95: 5. **Progressive difficulty** — start gentle so the player can learn, then ramp up. Don't dump them into chaos at t=0.\n96: 6. **No dead time** — something interesting on screen at all times. Idle background animation (stars, gradient pulse, particles) is fine.\n97: 7. **Persistent score / progress UI** — drawn on the canvas at the top corner, always visible.\n98: 8. **Audio (optional but encouraged)** — generate tones with `OscillatorNode` (no external assets). **Do not auto-start audio on page load** — browsers block this and it will throw. Initialize the `AudioContext` lazily on the first keypress or click. Wrap audio code in try/catch.\n99: \n100: ## Playable Contract — non-negotiable\n101: \n102: These are not aspirations. A game that violates any of these is broken even if it runs without errors. After your implementation finishes, the validator loads the page in a real browser and runs an 8-second playtest with synthetic input — failures here are reported back to you and must be fixed before the game ships.\n103: \n104: ### 1. The player can lose\n105: \n106: There must be a collision check (or other end condition) that fires `gameOver` and stops the active gameplay loop. \"Game runs forever, no death\" is broken. Examples that have failed in the past: collision check compared a value against itself; \"spike\" collision only clamped position instead of killing the player; obstacles never reached the player.\n107: \n108: ### 2. The player can score\n109: \n110: The score must increase on a **discrete event** (a survived obstacle, a collected pickup, an enemy destroyed, a beat hit). \"Score = elapsed seconds\" is allowed but weak. Score must be drawn on the canvas at all times.\n111: \n112: ### 3. The first 3 seconds are survivable with no input\n113: \n114: Define a `SAFE_RADIUS` (e.g. 200px) around the player's starting position. When spawning hazards, **reject any spawn that is within `SAFE_RADIUS` of the player AND would reach them within 3 seconds at current hazard speed**. Pre-spawning hazards on top of the player at t=0 has caused frame-1 deaths in past games. Don't do it.\n115: \n116: ### 4. The control actually changes the outcome\n117: \n118: Whatever the player presses must, in the next frame, change a value that affects collision or score. If a control is purely cosmetic (e.g. \"stretch horizontally\" but the gaps are vertical), the game is fundamentally broken even if it runs. After writing, **re-read the game's concept and trace one frame**: does the input do what the concept claims?\n119: \n120: ### 5. dt-based physics, not per-frame constants\n121: \n122: All movement and timing must scale by delta time (seconds since last frame). Per-frame constants (`x += 5` every frame) make games run 2.4× faster on a 144Hz display.\n123: \n124: ```js\n125: let lastTime = performance.now();\n126: function loop(now) {\n127:   const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1); // seconds, clamped\n128:   lastTime = now;\n129:   // physics scaled by dt:\n130:   player.x += player.vx * dt;\n131:   enemy.y  += enemy.vy  * dt;\n132:   spawnTimer -= dt;\n133:   // ...\n134:   requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n135: }\n136: requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n137: ```\n138: \n139: ### 6. That function MUST `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` the previous loop before scheduling a new one — otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game slows to a crawl over time.\n142: \n143: ```js\n144: let loopId = null;\n145: function startLoop() {\n146:   if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);\n147:   function tick(now) { /* ... */ loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); }\n148:   loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n149: }\n150: ```\n151: \n152: ### 7. Survive as long as possible.\", // 10-200 chars, one sentence\n170: };\n171: ```\n172: \n173: Rules:\n174: - The object must exist from the very first frame, before any input.\n175: - Update `state`, `score`, `player.x`, `player.y`, and `player.visible` whenever they change. prompt (13,902 chars)
## Reference example: "breakout"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Breaker",
  "description": "Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.",
  "controls": [
    "Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle",
    "Hold Space to launch faster",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "breakout", "bouncing"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Breaker</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Breaker</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle</li>
      <li>Hold Space to launch faster</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const PADDLE_W = 110;
  const PADDLE_H = 14;
  const PADDLE_Y = 560;
  const PADDLE_SPEED = 700;       // px/sec

  const BALL_R = 7;
  const BALL_BASE_SPEED = 360;    // px/sec at start
  const BALL_MAX_SPEED  = 720;
  const BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT = 6; // px/sec added per brick

  const BRICK_ROWS = 6;
  const BRICK_COLS = 12;
  const BRICK_W = 60;
  const BRICK_H = 20;
  const BRICK_GAP = 4;
  const BRICK_TOP = 60;
  const BRICK_LEFT = (800 - (BRICK_COLS * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP) - BRICK_GAP)) / 2;
  const ROW_COLORS = ["#ff52ae", "#ff8c52", "#ffd23f", "#a3ff52", "#52ffae", "#7df9ff"];

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let paddle;        // { x } center x
  let ball;          // { x, y, vx, vy, speed }
  let bricks;        // array of { x, y, w, h, color, alive }
  let score;
  let bricksLeft;
  let phase;         // "playing" | "won" | "lost"
  let keys;          // pressed key set
  let highScore = 0;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    paddle = { x: 400 };
    ball = { x: 400, y: 480, vx: 0, vy: 0, speed: BALL_BASE_SPEED };
    launchBall();
    bricks = [];
    for (let r = 0; r < BRICK_ROWS; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < BRICK_COLS; c++) {
        bricks.push({
          x: BRICK_LEFT + c * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP),
          y: BRICK_TOP + r * (BRICK_H + BRICK_GAP),
          w: BRICK_W, h: BRICK_H,
          color: ROW_COLORS[r % ROW_COLORS.length],
          alive: true,
        });
      }
    }
    bricksLeft = bricks.length;
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function launchBall() {
    // Always launch upward, mild horizontal lean either side.
    const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + (Math.random() * 0.6 - 0.3);
    ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
    ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Paddle.
    const dirX = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
               - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
    const speedMul = keys.has(" ") ? 1.6 : 1;
    paddle.x += dirX * PADDLE_SPEED * speedMul * dt;
    paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x, PADDLE_W / 2, canvas.width - PADDLE_W / 2);

    // Ball motion.
    ball.x += ball.vx * dt;
    ball.y += ball.vy * dt;

    // Walls.
    if (ball.x < BALL_R) { ball.x = BALL_R; ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.x > canvas.width - BALL_R) { ball.x = canvas.width - BALL_R; ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.y < BALL_R) { ball.y = BALL_R; ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy); beep(220, 0.04); }

    // Floor — lose.
    if (ball.y > canvas.height + BALL_R) {
      phase = "lost";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(110, 0.25);
      return;
    }

    // Paddle.
    const px = paddle.x, py = PADDLE_Y;
    if (ball.vy > 0
        && ball.y + BALL_R >= py
        && ball.y - BALL_R <= py + PADDLE_H
        && ball.x >= px - PADDLE_W / 2 - BALL_R
        && ball.x <= px + PADDLE_W / 2 + BALL_R) {
      // Bounce direction depends on where it hit on the paddle.
      const offset = (ball.x - px) / (PADDLE_W / 2); // -1..1
      const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + offset * 1.0;   // up-ish, leaning side
      ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.y = py - BALL_R - 0.1;
      beep(540, 0.05);
    }

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      if (ball.x + BALL_R < b.x || ball.x - BALL_R > b.x + b.w) continue;
      if (ball.y + BALL_R < b.y || ball.y - BALL_R > b.y + b.h) continue;

      b.alive = false;
      bricksLeft--;
      score += 10;
      ball.speed = Math.min(BALL_MAX_SPEED, ball.speed + BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT);

      // Decide which side was hit by comparing penetrations.
      const overlapX = Math.min(ball.x + BALL_R - b.x, b.x + b.w - (ball.x - BALL_R));
      const overlapY = Math.min(ball.y + BALL_R - b.y, b.y + b.h - (ball.y - BALL_R));
      if (overlapX < overlapY) ball.vx = -ball.vx; else ball.vy = -ball.vy;

      // Renormalize to current speed.
      const m = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
      ball.vx = (ball.vx / m) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = (ball.vy / m) * ball.speed;
      beep(880, 0.05);
      break;
    }

    if (bricksLeft === 0) {
      phase = "won";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(990, 0.2);
    }
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      ctx.fillRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
    }

    // Paddle.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.fillRect(paddle.x - PADDLE_W / 2, PADDLE_Y, PADDLE_W, PADDLE_H);

    // Ball.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, BALL_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Bricks ${bricksLeft}  ·  Best ${highScore}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "lost" || phase === "won") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = phase === "won" ? "#52ffae" : "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText(phase === "won" ? "You Win!" : "Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "square";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase === "playing" ? "playing" : "gameover",
      score,
      player: {
        x: paddle.x,
        y: PADDLE_Y + PADDLE_H / 2,
        visible: paddle.x >= 0 && paddle.x <= canvas.width,
      },
      objective: "Bounce the ball with the paddle to break every brick.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

```js
    // Minimal stub to avoid runtime errors
    const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const restartBtn = document.getElementById('restart');
    let loopId = null;
    let keys = new Set();
    
    function reset() {
      // Clear canvas
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      // Simple placeholder render
      ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2, 10, 0, Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fill();
      // Publish minimal observable state
      publishState();
    }
    
    function publishState() {
      window.__game = {
        state: 'menu',
        score: 0,
        player: { x: canvas.width/2, y: canvas.height/2, visible: true },
        objective: 'Toggle the dot’s radius to match tunnel gaps as they scroll by.'
      };
    }
    
    document.addEventListener('keydown', ev => {
      if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener('keyup', ev => keys.delete(ev.key));
    restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);
    
    // Initial setup
    reset();
```

```js
// Minimal stub to avoid runtime errors
const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
const restartBtn = document.getElementById('restart');
let loopId = null;
let keys = new Set();

function reset() {
  // Clear canvas
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  // Simple placeholder render
  ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2, 10, 0, Math.PI*2);
  ctx.fill();
  // Publish minimal observable state
  publishState();
}

function publishState() {
  window.__game = {
    state: 'menu',
    score: 0,
    player: { x: canvas.width/2, y: canvas.height/2, visible: true },
    objective: 'Toggle the dot’s radius to match tunnel gaps as they scroll by.'
  };
}

document.addEventListener('keydown', ev => {
  if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
  keys.add(ev.key);
});
document.addEventListener('keyup', ev => keys.delete(ev.key));
restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);

// Initial setup
reset();
```

Now need new script content with sections.

We'll create:

```js
    // === CONFIG =============================================================

    const PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL = 5;
    const PLAYER_RADIUS_LARGE = 12;
    const SAFE_RADIUS = 200;
    const GAP_SMALL = PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL;
    const GAP_LARGE = PLAYER_RADIUS_LARGE;

    // === STATE =============================================================

    const canvas = document.getElementById('game');
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const restartBtn = document.getElementById('restart');
    let loopId = null;
    let keys = new Set();

    let player = { x: canvas.width/2, y: canvas.height/2, radius: PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL, targetRadius: PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL, visible: true };
    let hazards = [];
    let score = 0;
    let phase = "menu";
    let lastTime = performance.now();
    let audioCtx = null;

    // === RESET =============================================================

    function reset() {
      // Initialize game state
      player.x = canvas.width/2;
      player.y = canvas.height/2;
      player.radius = PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL;
      player.targetRadius = PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL;
      player.visible = true;
      hazards = [];
      score = 0;
      phase = "playing";
      // Clear canvas
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      // Draw placeholder player
      ctx.fillStyle = '#7df9ff';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fill();
      // Publish state
      publishState();
    }

    // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================

    function publishState() {
      window.__game = {
        state: phase,
        score: score,
        player: { x: player.x, y: player.y, visible: player.visible },
        objective: 'Toggle the dot’s radius to match tunnel gaps as they scroll by.'
      };
    }

    // === INPUT =============================================================

    document.addEventListener('keydown', ev => {
      if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
      // Toggle radius on any key (placeholder)
      if (ev.key === ' ') {
        // toggle radius between small and large
        player.targetRadius = (player.targetRadius === PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL) ? PLAYER_RADIUS_LARGE : PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL;
        // Apply instantly for now
        player.radius = player.targetRadius;
      }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener('keyup', ev => keys.delete(ev.key));
    restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);

    // === BOOT ===============================================================

    reset();
```

  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;

}

// === LOOP/UPDATE =============================================================
// startLoop implementation

function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      // Update logic placeholder
      // (none for now)

      // Render (clear and draw player)
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      // Publish observable state
      publishState();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("loop error", err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

// === RENDER =============================================================
// Placeholder render function, may be expanded later
function render() {
  // Clear and draw player (called by startLoop)
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
}

function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      // Clear canvas
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      // Render player placeholder
      ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      // Could also render score etc. but minimal
      publishState();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("loop error", err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

So modify to:

```
    // === INPUT =============================================================
    // Keyboard tracking
    document.addEventListener('keydown', ev => {
      if (ev.key === 'r' || ev.key === 'R') { reset(); return; }
      // Toggle radius on space
      if (ev.key === ' ') {
        player.targetRadius = (player.targetRadius === PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL) ? PLAYER_RADIUS_LARGE : PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL;
        player.radius = player.targetRadius;
      }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener('keyup', ev => keys.delete(ev.key));

    // Mouse / touch input for radius toggle
    // Click or tap on the canvas toggles the player's radius
    canvas.addEventListener('click', () => {
      player.targetRadius = (player.targetRadius === PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL) ? PLAYER_RADIUS_LARGE : PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL;
      player.radius = player.targetRadius;
    });
    canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', ev => {
      ev.preventDefault(); // prevent phantom mouse events
      player.targetRadius = (player.targetRadius === PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL) ? PLAYER_RADIUS_LARGE : PLAYER_RADIUS_SMALL;
      player.radius = player.targetRadius;
    }, { passive: false });

    // Restart button
    restartBtn.addEventListener('click', reset);
```

We need to ensure that we use `canvas` variable defined earlier. It is available.

function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancel...;
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      // Update
      if (phase === "playing") {
         // forward motion
         player.x += PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
         // spawn timer
         spawnTimer -= dt;
         if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
            const gapSize = Math.random() < 0.5 ? GAP_SMALL : GAP_LARGE;
            const spawnX = player.x + canvas.width + Math.random() * 200;
            hazards.push({ x: spawnX, size: gapSize, passed: false });
            spawnTimer = 1 + Math.random() * 2; // 1-3 sec
         }
         // check hazards
         for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const gap = hazards[i];
            if (!gap.passed && player.x >= gap.x) {
               gap.passed = true;
               if (player.radius === gap.size) {
                  score++;
               } else {
                  phase = "gameover";
               }
               hazards.splice(i, 1);
            }
         }
      }
      // Clear canvas
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      // Basic render placeholder: draw player dot
      ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(player.x - player.x + canvas.width/2, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2); // maybe keep center?
      // Actually we have player.x moving forward; we want drawn at fixed screen x (center) maybe.
      // To keep them visible, we might draw the player at a fixed screen x, like canvas.width/4.
      // For simplicity: draw at canvas.width/4 constant x coordinate.
```

We'll define:

```
    function startLoop() {
      // Cancel any existing animation frame to avoid stacking loops
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          // Update game state if playing
          if (phase === "playing") {
            // Advance player forward
            player.x += PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
            // Update spawn timer and spawn gaps
            spawnTimer -= dt;
            if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
              const gapSize = Math.random() < 0.5 ? GAP_SMALL : GAP_LARGE;
              const spawnX = player.x + canvas.width + Math.random() * 200;
              hazards.push({ x: spawnX, size: gapSize, passed: false });
              // Next spawn in 1-3 seconds
              spawnTimer = 1 + Math.random() * 2;
            }
            // Check for collisions / scoring
            for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
              const gap = hazards[i];
              if (!gap.passed && player.x >= gap.x) {
                gap.passed = true;
                if (player.radius === gap.size) {
                  score++;
                } else {
                  phase = "gameover";
                }
                hazards.splice(i, 1);
              }
            }
          }
          // Clear canvas each frame
          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
          // Basic render placeholder: draw player dot at fixed screen position
          const screenX = canvas.width * 0.25;
          ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(screenX, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
          // Publish observable state each frame
          publishState();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("loop error", err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
```

prompt (5,135 chars)
Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Scale Shift
Concept: Tap to toggle the dot’s radius between a tiny and a large size; each tunnel gap is sized for one radius, so you must switch sizes at the right moments to thread the openings.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.
